-- Q8 deployed code, which initialise package variant is called, trigger enabled
SELECT object_name, object_type, status, last_ddl_time FROM ALL_OBJECTS
WHERE owner='FCIPAYROLL'
  AND object_name IN ('TRG_CANCEL_LEAVE','PKG_INITIAL_PAYROLL_FCI','PKG_INITIAL_PAYROLL_FCI_V1');
  
Insert into EXPORT_TABLE (OBJECT_NAME,OBJECT_TYPE,STATUS,LAST_DDL_TIME) values ('PKG_INITIAL_PAYROLL_FCI','PACKAGE','VALID',to_date('08-05-26','DD-MM-RR'));
Insert into EXPORT_TABLE (OBJECT_NAME,OBJECT_TYPE,STATUS,LAST_DDL_TIME) values ('PKG_INITIAL_PAYROLL_FCI','PACKAGE BODY','VALID',to_date('17-04-26','DD-MM-RR'));
Insert into EXPORT_TABLE (OBJECT_NAME,OBJECT_TYPE,STATUS,LAST_DDL_TIME) values ('PKG_INITIAL_PAYROLL_FCI_V1','PACKAGE','VALID',to_date('08-05-26','DD-MM-RR'));
Insert into EXPORT_TABLE (OBJECT_NAME,OBJECT_TYPE,STATUS,LAST_DDL_TIME) values ('PKG_INITIAL_PAYROLL_FCI_V1','PACKAGE BODY','VALID',to_date('24-02-26','DD-MM-RR'));
Insert into EXPORT_TABLE (OBJECT_NAME,OBJECT_TYPE,STATUS,LAST_DDL_TIME) values ('TRG_CANCEL_LEAVE','TRIGGER','VALID',to_date('27-08-25','DD-MM-RR'));

-- Q9 other employees affected (cancelled after processing, no reversal row)
SELECT i.emp_no, i.emp_leave_id, i.leave_typ, i.cancel_date, r.rec_amt, r.lve_pros_yymm
FROM FCIPAYROLL.PAY_LEAVE_INFO i
JOIN FCIPAYROLL.PAY_LEAVE_REC_DTLS r
  ON r.lveappl_id = i.emp_leave_id AND r.emp_num = i.emp_no
WHERE i.cancel_flag = 'C'
  AND i.leave_typ IN ('LVTYP$HPL','LVTYP$EOL','LVTYP$EOLMC','LVTYP$CCL')
  AND r.rec_amt IS NOT NULL AND r.lve_pros_yymm IS NOT NULL AND r.rec_yymm IS NOT NULL
  AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM FCIPAYROLL.PAY_LEAVE_CHANGE c
                  WHERE c.leave_appl_id = i.emp_leave_id)
                  and i.emp_no = 314348;
                  
                  
Insert into EXPORT_TABLE (EMP_NO,EMP_LEAVE_ID,LEAVE_TYP,CANCEL_DATE,REC_AMT,LVE_PROS_YYMM) values (314348,34543700,'LVTYP$HPL',to_date('10-10-26','DD-MM-RR'),3084,202609);
